## Architecture & systems

- 'Claim' in the cash flow system refers to commission for a closed lead. [CONFIRMED · fathom/135708347 @ [00:14:47] Abhijeet Gandhi: So maybe it's also that someone has lead closed. And then claim means that it is It's commission.]
- A 'Days Taken' numeric field is used to aggregate (sum or average) progress across records; at the time of review some records showed zero, making the aggregate zero. [CONFIRMED · fathom/128933093 @ 07:27 – Yogita: 'Let's just sum it' / 07:29 Abhijeet: 'So it's zero… Because it's zero here']
- A 'Dills' channel exists containing client tables, with leads tables and deal-related views being configured or reviewed during this session. [CONFIRMED · fathom/136607733 @ 00:01:00]
- A 'Light Closer' team filter is being added to the existing interface; the underlying automations remain unchanged and only the interface layer is modified with two filters: Closer and Team. [CONFIRMED · fathom/151365604 @ 00:00:06 – 'राधर देन बैट वी कान अड़ फिल्ट काल्ड एस टीम... बस दो फिल्टर लगा देंगी कि लोजर और टीम']
- A 'Progress' view is being built that filters records by coach and displays Days Taken per milestone record; clicking into a record shows fields related to the coach linked to that progress entry. [CONFIRMED · fathom/128933093 @ 02:05 – Yogita: 'जब अपन उसके अंदर क्लिक इंटू रेकॉर्ड करेंगे, तो वो प्रोग्रेस, जिस कोच का रिलेटर है, उसका फिल्स दिखाएंगे']
- A 'Send Notification' automation also exists in ZTH (closed state) that triggers when a record is created in the Notifications table, but had condition errors preventing it from firing correctly. [CONFIRMED · fathom/120740444 @ 00:03:31 – 'there is actually an automation which is basically by the name of Send Notification, which is closed... when the record is created in Notification, it's all being sent... what is the error conditions are made']
- A 'Start Onboarding' button triggers an automation to create a delivery record, but a condition blocking creation when contract status is already 'signed' was identified as a bug; the fix is to remove that status condition. [CONFIRMED · fathom/125898289 @ 00:05:37]
- A 'closer' tag assignment feature is being built in Airtable for the client MC; one closer has been provided so far, with more to be added next week. [CONFIRMED · fathom/152182045 @ 00:08:50 - 'they have given us like one closer to which we want to assign like a light closer tag and they said next week there are going to be more people that they will provide']
- A 'days in phase' column per milestone record is proposed to be maintained alongside the new-record-per-milestone approach, to enable dashboard-level metric display of time spent in each phase. [CONFIRMED · fathom/128900490 @ maybe we need to do both for example right]
- A 'days in phase' value will be calculated by the script (not as a formula field) by computing the difference between the creation date of the previous milestone record and the creation date of the new record, then storing that value in a new dedicated column (e.g. 'Script Calculator' or similar name). [CONFIRMED · fathom/128905225 @ 00:06:00]

And then, for the other ones, they are not there']
- A bug was identified where a Typeform submission lacked an email field, causing the automation to fail to find or save the contact record correctly; a fix was applied by adding an email field to the form/automation. [CONFIRMED · fathom/166892206 @ 00:17:25]
- A bug was identified where a cash flow record had an empty 'payment product ID' field, causing the Stripe payment link generation to fail with the error 'cannot generate record, payment product ID, missing required fields'. [CONFIRMED · fathom/118066387 @ 00:03:35]
- A bug was identified where a script uses an auto-scope variable (instead of a passed parameter) to get the payment record ID, causing it to be overwritten when multiple payments are processed simultaneously — whichever payment runs last overwrites the payment record ID for all. [CONFIRMED · fathom/137182298 @ Critical work found in this script, the problem is not passed to the parameter, it's using the autoscope variable to get it on the top of the script]
- A bug was identified where the Typeform webhook triggers twice on a single submission — once for a partial response and once for the completed response — causing duplicate records to be created in the system. [CONFIRMED · fathom/143292093 @ 00:35:17]
- A button is being added to allow users to create a new metric record directly; the flow opens a pop-up for the user to fill in details rather than leaving an empty record. [CONFIRMED · fathom/142871800 @ 00:11:24]
- A centrally maintained list (in the script) will define the ordered steps/milestones. When a step is complete, the script identifies the current step number and creates a new record with the next step as 'Milestone Pending'. Editing or deleting a step only requires changing the main list; all downstream logic updates automatically. [CONFIRMED · fathom/128905225 @ 00:04:15]
